# DECISIONS.md — DODDY V2 decision log with status

Companion to the pack's proposal table `docs/architecture/DECISIONS.md` (left unmodified).
Status values: **Applied in M0 — owner approval pending**, **Not exercised in M0**, **Open**. Nothing here is
owner-approved; the owner marks each `accepted / rejected / needs investigation`. Dates are 2026-10-08.

**Numbers used twice** (found 18 Oct, ADR-520; 217 added 19 Oct): 217 (voice and place reminder screens; list follow-ups), 218 (repeating reminders; photo notes) and 458 (stale-session guard; screens in more languages). Cite those by number and title. 497–499 (#84), 500–507 (#90), 510 (#85) and 520–526 are on main since 19 Oct. Open pull requests hold 530 (#89, not 490) and 540 (#96). #86 and `claude/epic-wright-vz93xh` (its commit `3dd1a00d`) reuse 483, which is ASK-01 on main, and #92 (`claude/awesome-cray-2lb9ia`) reuses 493–495, which are #84's on main; those need new numbers before they merge. New rows start at 541 or above until they settle. The v1 scope rows below were first numbered 500–502 and moved to 520–522 on 18 October to stay clear of #90; commits from before the move use the old numbers. 541–544 are the owner's 19 Oct decisions (#101).

## Pack ADRs (001–012)

| ADR | Status after M0 | Evidence / notes |
|---|---|---|
| 001 Greenfield repo, old repo read-only | Applied in M0 — approval pending | Work only in `Bienos/doddy.2`; old repo not touched or cloned. |
| 002 iOS 17+, SwiftUI, Swift Concurrency | Applied — approval pending | `project.yml` iOS 17.0, iPhone only, Swift 6 mode. Built and tested on Xcode 27.0 / iOS simulator on 2026-10-08. |
| 003 GRDB/SQLite local-first | Applied in M1 — approval pending | `DoddyPersistence/SQLiteMemoryRepository` (GRDB 7.9.0, migration `v1_memories`, FTS5). Exercised against real SQLite on Linux. |
| 004 Exact original text + immutable provenance | Applied in domain model, unit-tested | `MemoryRecord.rawText` immutable, `correcting(to:at:)` keeps raw text and capture date; 13 tests. |
| 005 Lexical/FTS retrieval first | Applied in M1 | FTS5 prefix-OR query ranked by bm25 then matched-term count; no embeddings. |
| 006 Provider-neutral AI, server-side key proxy | Not exercised in M0 | `AssistantClient` port + `UnavailableAssistantClient`; no provider, no key, no network code (guarded by script). |
| 007 Typed tool schema, confirmation, receipts | Port only | `ToolProposal` → `ToolConfirmation` → `ToolExecutor.execute` → `ActionReceipt`; no executor exists. |
| 008 No account/sync/Postgres | Applied in M0 | None exist; `PrivacyDefaults.m0` all off. |
| 009 Persona separate from safety/data/action policy | Not exercised in M0 | No persona or model in M0. |
| 010 Choose ONE brand ZIP after art QA | **Open — blocks any "final brand" claim** | UI shows only Pocket Notebook candidate tokens and 2 candidate PNGs, labelled "Candidate art — not final". Both v1.3 ZIPs are preserved untouched. |
| 011 Content-free telemetry, separate control | Applied in M0 (no telemetry at all) | Settings states analytics/telemetry "Off (not built)". |
| 012 `reference/` never overrides canonical docs | Applied | `reference/` is outside every build target. |

## Architecture changes and decisions made while implementing M0

| ADR | Decision | Why | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 013 | The Xcode project is generated from `project.yml` (XcodeGen); `Doddy.xcodeproj` is **not committed**. | No Xcode here to open/validate a project; hand-writing a pbxproj would be unverifiable. Spec was validated by generating with XcodeGen 2.44.1 on Linux only. | Applied — approval pending; revisit after first Mac build |
| 014 | Domain code lives in a local Swift package `Packages/DoddyCore` (targets `DoddyCore`, `DoddyDesignTokens`) rather than only app folders. **Deviation** from `IOS_ARCHITECTURE.md`'s "folders/modules, no multi-package". | The doc allows splitting "where tests/build benefit": it is the only part that can compile and be tested without a Mac. App code stays in `DoddyApp/`. One package, two targets — not a monorepo. | Applied — approval pending |
| 015 | Swift language mode 6, tools-version 6.0; typed throws in domain validation. | Strict concurrency from the start; package verified on Swift 6.3.3 (Linux) and 6.4 (macOS); app target built in Swift 6 mode on Xcode 27.0. | Applied — approval pending |
| 016 | Text limit counted in **Unicode scalars** (matches JSON Schema `maxLength`); whitespace-only = empty; accepted text stored **exactly** (no trim, no normalisation). | Pack says "5,000 Unicode characters" — ambiguous between scalars and grapheme clusters; schema counts code points. A test pins the difference (family emoji = 7 scalars). | Applied — **owner to confirm** |
| 017 | `MemoryRecord` does not model `deletedAtMs` tombstones; deletion is a purge. Schema keeps the field; a test asserts this is the only difference. | `MEMORY_AND_DATA.md` leaves tombstone policy undefined ("define purge policy"); safest default for erasure. | Applied — **owner to decide before M1** |
| 018 | Design tokens come from the **Pocket Notebook** table in `design/interface-v1.2/docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`, not `assets/brand_candidate/`. Tokens live in pure-Swift `DoddyDesignTokens`, WCAG contrast unit-tested (text roles ≥ 4.5:1 in light and dark; figures match `contrast.json`). | `CANONICAL_DESIGN_DIRECTION.md` precedence rule #5. Bright orange is decoration only. | Applied as **candidate** — approval pending |
| 019 | `@AppStorage`/`UserDefaults` is used **only** for three UI preferences (appearance, hide character artwork, has-seen-First-Hello); never for memories. `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` declares reason CA92.1. | Old prototype's `UserDefaults` memory store is the thing to avoid; UI prefs are a different class. | Applied — approval pending |
| 020 | Honesty by construction: `CapabilityCatalog.m0` (every capability "not built"), `Unavailable*` adapters that throw, UI copy derived from the catalog, and `scripts/check_forbidden_capabilities.sh` (no networking/audio/speech/EventKit/account code, no secrets, no permission keys). | Makes "no fake capability" testable instead of a promise. | Applied — approval pending |
| 021 | "Accessibility toggle" is **Hide character artwork** (real effect), not a reduce-motion switch. | M0 has no animation, so a motion toggle would do nothing (a fake). System Reduce Motion stays authoritative later. | Applied — approval pending |
| 022 | No GitHub Actions workflow; checks run via `scripts/ci.sh`. | Hosted macOS runners are billable; M0 forbids paid services without approval. | **Superseded by ADR-123** (hosted macOS build approved 2026-10-10) |
| 023 | Dev bundle id `com.bienos.doddy2.dev`, version 0.0.1, no signing team, no app icon (icon: superseded by ADR-050). | Production identifier/team/icon are owner decisions; placeholders are clearly development-only. | Open |
| 024 | Imported pack files unmodified; a second checker `scripts/verify_imported_pack.py` was added because `tools/verify_pack.py` rejects any file the V2 repo adds (it also hashes itself, so it was not edited). | Keep provenance intact. | Applied |

## M1 decisions (local memory loop)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 025 | Owner instruction 2026-10-08: build M1 now, **no unnecessary tests**. M1 adds 2 storage tests only; UI flows are untested beyond the existing smoke tests. | Applied |
| 026 | `MemoryRepository` gains `list(limit:)` and `deleteAll()`; new `MemoryStoreError`. Every write is one transaction plus read-back before success. | Applied |
| 027 | Search indexes a folded copy of `current_text` (`search_text`: case + diacritics folded, plus ł→l, ß→ss, etc.). Query = non-stopword terms as FTS5 prefix terms joined with OR. No typo tolerance beyond prefixes. | Applied — owner may want fuzzier search later — **extended by ADR-093** |
| 028 | `PRAGMA secure_delete = ON`; delete-all also rebuilds the FTS index. DB in Application Support/Memories with `completeUntilFirstUserAuthentication`; **included in device backups** (disclosed in Settings). | Applied — **owner confirmed 2026-10-09** |
| 029 | If the store cannot open, the app switches saving off and shows the error; it never resets or deletes the file. | Applied |
| 030 | Export: JSON (`formatVersion` 1 envelope of schema records) and plain text, both via the share sheet. | Applied |
| 031 | On Linux, package tests need `-Xswiftc -DSQLITE_DISABLE_SNAPSHOT` (Ubuntu SQLite lacks the snapshot API); `ci.sh`/`Makefile` add it automatically. Apple builds unaffected. | Applied |
| 032 | Not built in M1: private/no-history session mode (PRD item 6), content-free telemetry (item 8), conflict highlighting beyond listing all matches. | Private mode built (ADR-131); conflict highlighting built for differing numbers (ADR-132); content-free telemetry still open — owner to decide |

## V1 voice decisions

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 033 | Voice runs on **ElevenLabs Agents** (owner has Creator plan), behind app-side tool handling; provider bake-off vs OpenAI Realtime still recommended | Applied (owner instruction) |
| 034 | Dev agent is **public**; its ID is in `VoiceConfig.swift`. Token server + authenticated agent required before any other user | Applied for dev — **must change before TestFlight** |
| 035 | Agent tools are proposals: search runs locally; save needs an on-screen tap; agent is told saved, declined or failed | Applied |
| 036 | Voice is foreground-only in V1; background listening + Dynamic Island are V3 | Superseded by ADR-053 (V3) |
| 037 | Guard script: no networking of our own, audio and ElevenLabs code only in `Features/Voice`, microphone is the only permission | Applied; the permission rule is superseded by 063 |
| 038 | Doddy speaks with the ElevenLabs Voice Library voice **Josh – Conversational Agent** `ZoiZ8fuDWInAcwPXaVeq` for both languages (added to My Voices and set on the dev agent 2026-10-08, version `agtvrsn_2401m4eyzmrwepct8ftekgsqtv3z`); no per-language voice override | Applied (owner instruction) — Polish pronunciation to be judged on device; library licence terms to be checked before release |
| 039 | Before `save_memory` runs, the agent says only "Confirm on screen if I should save this." / "Potwierdź na ekranie, jeśli mam to zapisać." (prompt + tool description, agent version `agtvrsn_1401m4f01rzheztb1sp89ek1wy14`, 2026-10-09) instead of "I'm saving this for you" | Applied (owner approved) — not yet heard on a device |
| 045 | Recall mismatch seen in the device log (English question, Polish note; code saved as number words): the agent now searches with key words in **both** Polish and English, numbers as digits, retries once, and saves numbers/codes/dates as digits (tool descriptions + prompt, agent version `agtvrsn_2701m4f1e2krfnysbc5w01agz5d2`, 2026-10-09). The app's lexical search is unchanged | Applied — to be exercised on a device |
| 046 | **Personal single-user build** (owner instruction 2026-10-09: "design it for me only, it must have knowledge"). Doddy's knowledge = the owner's saved notes and recent conversations, sent by the app at conversation start as dynamic variables; the multi-user privacy posture (minimal egress) is relaxed for the owner's own phone and stated in the UI | Applied — reverses the "only looked-up notes leave the device" rule for this build |
| 047 | Cross-conversation memory is kept **on the phone**: last 10 transcripts as JSON (`ConversationLogStore`), toggle + forget in Settings, never memory records. ElevenLabs user memory was refused on the Creator plan | Applied |
| 048 | Weather (Open-Meteo) and facts (Wikipedia pl/en) as keyless ElevenLabs webhook tools, executed from ElevenLabs servers. No general web search until a search key is approved | Applied — owner may add a search provider |
| 049 | A random stable per-install id is sent as the ElevenLabs conversation `user_id`; agent `trust_context` set to high (serves its owner) | Applied |
| 050 | **Candidate app icon** (owner request 2026-10-09): Doddy waving, cropped to head and hand from `design/interface-v1.2/assets/doddy-pose-waving-*-v1.3-r1.svg`; light = ink on orange `#F58A24` (spark recoloured to ink), dark = cream on `#171F29`. 1024 px, no alpha, light + dark appearances; `ASSETCATALOG_COMPILER_APPICON_NAME` back to `AppIcon` | Applied — candidate, not a brand master; tinted variant not made |
| 051 | Agent voice set to `oDULUm0GMltHA6mVZkWh` (owner instruction 2026-10-09), replacing Josh `ZoiZ8fuDWInAcwPXaVeq` (ADR-038); one voice for EN and PL | Applied — agent version `agtvrsn_2701m4fymws8f6wama5zd3zxn1w5` |
| 052 | "Not smart" fix (owner report 2026-10-09): LLM `gemini-2.5-flash` → `gemini-3.5-flash`, temperature 0.3 → 0.5; prompt answers general questions from own knowledge, allows up to 6 sentences when asked to explain, natural small talk; weather (`geocode_place`, `get_weather`) and Wikipedia (`wikipedia_search`, `wikipedia_summary`) webhook tools **re-attached** (a parallel calendar edit had replaced the tool list and dropped them, so Doddy told the owner it couldn't do weather). Calendar/reminder/contact tools and strict honesty rules kept; `{{saved_notes}}`/`{{recent_conversations}}` sections kept with their placeholders | Applied — agent version `agtvrsn_0901m4fynwpdfv0agh5qqn1ytyxd`; not yet heard on a device; per-minute LLM cost may rise |

## V3 decisions (Dynamic Island, 2026-10-09)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 053 | Owner request "now live island": a conversation keeps running when the user leaves the app (background audio mode, `Config/Doddy-Info.plist`) and shows a **Live Activity** (new `DoddyWidgets` widget extension): Doddy's head, status mirrored from SDK events (connecting / listening / thinking / speaking / "Confirm in Doddy" while a save waits / muted), elapsed time, **Mute** and **End** buttons (`LiveActivityIntent`s that run in the app). Reverses ADR-036's "ends on background". The activity ends immediately with the conversation and stale ones are cleared at launch. A conversation still has to start in the foreground (iOS rule) | Applied — unbuilt; needs device testing (background audio kill reports on recent iOS, see research §3) |
| 054 | Live Activities are optional: if the user disables them for Doddy the conversation still runs in the background and iOS shows its own microphone indicator. No push updates, no App Group, no data in the extension beyond the status it is given | Applied |
| 055 | The island may never outlive the app's knowledge of the conversation. Every activity update carries a **stale date** (90 s) and a heartbeat (every 40 s, `VoiceLiveActivity`) pushes it forward while the conversation is alive; the status itself still changes only on SDK events. Once stale (iOS ended the app in the background, a crash) the widget shows "Lost Doddy — open the app" with only a Dismiss button, and the End/Dismiss intent ends any remaining activity even when no conversation handler exists in the process. Follows the research rule "must stop claiming listening the moment audio stops" (`docs/research/VOICE_SHORTCUT_ISLAND_TOOLS_2026-10.md` §3) | Applied — unbuilt; needs device testing |
| 106 | Live Activity matches the owner-reviewed mockups (2026-10-10, "go build"): the **Lock Screen card follows light/dark** (paper `#FFF7E9` / Night surface `#252E39`, ink, secondary, accent, confirmed and error roles as colour sets in `DoddyWidgets/Assets.xcassets`); the Dynamic Island stays black and always uses the dark art. Character art is the owner's **approved numbered pack** (`UploadedDoddy_Listening`, `_Kept` after a save, `_Sitting` otherwise; the widget carries @2x/@3x downscales (160/240 px tall, Lanczos, no crop or edit) of the ZIP files the app ships byte-identical, because Live Activity images must be small) on a paper backing on the island and in dark mode; the derived `DoddyHead` crop is removed (owner: "only use correct assets"). Orange means "live and can hear you" only: muted shows mic-slash with Unmute as the main button, no orange. After End or a lost connection the activity ends with an **"Conversation ended" card** (notes actually saved, total time, Open Doddy) for 2 min on the Lock Screen; failures still remove it at once. No conversation text in the activity: the mockup's "last line" in the expanded island was dropped for that reason. | Applied — unbuilt; needs device testing |
| 107 | The Codex memory-journey step (`codex/doddy2-native-baseline`, owner-approved "build") edited `docs/design/CANONICAL_DESIGN_DIRECTION.md`, part of the imported pack, so `verify_imported_pack.py` failed (also on the Codex branch itself). As in ADR-091, `PACKAGE_MANIFEST.json` was refreshed for exactly that one file; every other pack file is still checked byte-for-byte. | Applied |

## V2 decisions (shortcut and guided Back Tap setup, 2026-10-09)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 040 | "Talk to Doddy" ships as an App Intent + App Shortcut (`openAppWhenRun`, iOS 17 target). The guided Back Tap setup is **optional**, reachable from a Home card and Settings, not inserted before First Hello as design v1.2 proposes; `CANONICAL_DESIGN_DIRECTION.md` and the build-order doc say skippable/deferred and "do not force the wizard" | Applied — owner may move it first-first later |
| 041 | Setup completion is `userReportedTested` only: an intent receipt inside a 120 s test window plus the user's own "I used Double Back Tap". No `verified` state exists because iOS exposes no Back Tap API; stale, early, duplicate and out-of-test receipts are refused; "confirm" without a receipt is refused (unit-tested in `BackTapSetupTests`) | Applied |
| 042 | `ShortcutRouter` is the single owner of the voice and setup presentations (moved out of `HomeView`), so a second invocation focuses the open conversation rather than starting another | Applied |
| 043 | Setup state persisted in UserDefaults as JSON (`backTapSetup`): outcome + active test window only, never transcripts or reported pages. Extends ADR-019's "UI preferences only" rule; an unexpired window is restored after a relaunch so a background-killed app still accepts the tap-test receipt | Applied — owner to confirm |
| 044 | `LaunchSmokeTests` corrected: since V1 the Settings microphone row reads "Only while talking", not "Not used" (the old assertion would have failed on the first Mac run of `scripts/ci.sh`) | Applied |

## Brand guideline decisions (2026-10-09, renumbered from 038–039 at branch consolidation)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 071 | `docs/design/BRAND_GUIDELINES.md` consolidates voice, product-copy, typography, colour-summary and mark rules from interface v1.2, the Character Bible and the two v1.3 brand archives. Precedence: owner-approved guidelines → `CANONICAL_DESIGN_DIRECTION.md` → this guideline for copy and type → `DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` for tokens. It records eight owner decisions (display typeface Caveat vs Lobster Two vs bespoke lettering; bundling a font; wordmark outlines; brand master palette; memory/detail/note terminology; Polish register and tagline; app icon; humour default) and recommends Caveat, informal Polish, "memory" for the record and "detail" for the content. | Proposed — **owner to review section 10** |
| 072 | Pack files (`README.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/design/*` from the handoff) stay unmodified so `verify_imported_pack.py` keeps passing (ADR-024); the guideline is linked from the repo-authored `docs/design/README.md` and this log instead of from the pack's README and `CLAUDE.md`. If the owner prefers in-place edits, the manifest hashes must be regenerated in the same change and ADR-024 revised. | Applied — owner may revise |

## Connections decisions (2026-10-09, renumbered from 038–040 at branch consolidation)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 063 | Owner choice 2026-10-09: calendar (read, add with Add tap), reminders (add with Add tap) and contacts (name lookup, details on screen only) are built behind a Settings → Connections card screen (with in-call "Turn on" prompts), all **off by default**. Turning one on asks iOS for permission (calendar and reminders full access, contacts). Only `Infrastructure/Connections` imports EventKit or Contacts; the guard enforces this and allows only those four permission keys. Not compiled for iOS yet (no Xcode here). | Applied — verify on Mac |
| 064 | Weather and Wikipedia are ElevenLabs webhook tools: ElevenLabs makes those requests, so they cannot be switched off from the phone. Alarms (AlarmKit, iOS 26+ and a new permission) are not added. | Applied |
| 065 | Owner choice 2026-10-09: timers (local notification, notification permission), maps (opens Apple Maps after a tap), Shortcuts (asks to run a named shortcut after a tap) and messages and email (drafts a text or email, opened in Messages or Mail for the user to send; needs Contacts). Contact card gains Call, Text and Email buttons, each a tap. Doddy never sends a message or places a call. No new Info.plist usage strings. Live agent gained four client tools and a prompt update. | Applied — verify on Mac |

## Voice token server decisions (2026-10-09, renumbered from 044–048 at branch consolidation)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 066 | `server/voice-token`: one stateless endpoint that swaps a tester access code for an ElevenLabs conversation token. Plain JS, web-standard handler, no dependencies; Vercel entry included | Applied (code); **not deployed** |
| 067 | Testers authenticate with per-person access codes (`label:code`, ≥16 chars, revocable by removing the entry), not accounts. Codes kept in the iOS Keychain, this device only | Applied: owner may later want App Attest or accounts |
| 068 | Spending cap = the ElevenLabs agent's own concurrency/daily call limits; the server keeps no counters | Open: **owner sets the limits** |
| 069 | App: a token server address switches voice to private mode; an invalid address or missing code never falls back to the public agent. Public agent stays as the dev default until the owner enables agent authentication | Applied |
| 070 | The app's only networking of its own is `VoiceTokenClient` (guard-enforced); it sends the access code, nothing about the user | Applied |

Onboarding's `SetupGuideView`, `OpenDoddyIntent` and Settings "Getting started" were removed; first use is now the optional introduction only (`ProfileStore.route` skips the core's setup step, whose types and tests stay in DoddyCore unused by the app). | Applied — owner may want Back Tap back as a first-use step; that would mean showing V2's guide inside onboarding |

Control Center control from `busy-mendel` and the OpenAI "Ask" tab with Supabase gateway from `busy-hawking` were not merged. | Applied — owner to decide whether any of those come back |
| 091 | The 'keep the taps quiet' design step (`gifted-hypatia`, owner request) edited 8 files of the imported design prototype in `design/interface-v1.2/` (its manifest, README, setup guide doc, `setup.js`, `studio.css/js`, verification files), which made `verify_imported_pack.py` fail. `PACKAGE_MANIFEST.json` hashes were refreshed for exactly those 8 files, so the check passes again; every other pack file is still byte-identical. Partial exception to ADR-024 | Applied — owner to confirm; revert = restore the 16 manifest lines and the design commit |
| 092 | The approved introduction answers can be sent to the voice agent as `{{user_profile}}` (`ProfilePrompt.render`: fixed labels, user's words on one line each, only saved fields). **Off by default**: Settings → About you → "Use in voice conversations"; when off the agent gets a fixed not-shared line. Home, About you and the Settings privacy note say which. Personality (ADR-062) and the profile sections were added to the live prompt the same day; the repo prompt (`voice-agent/instructions.md`) was also brought up to date with the live timers/maps/Shortcuts/drafts text | Applied 2026-10-09 (owner "build" on the recommendation) — owner switches it on; not yet heard on a device |
| 093 | Recall word rules (`RecallMatching`, DoddyCore), same for the Memories search and the voice `search_memories` tool: besides prefix match, a query word also matches its Polish/English counterpart from a fixed list of ~50 everyday groups, spoken numbers EN/PL 0–100 as digits (and runs of single numbers joined, so "5678" finds "pięć sześć siedem osiem"), a dropped ending (1 letter for 6–8 letters, 2 from 9), and one typo from 5 letters / two from 8. FTS5 runs first with every form; if it finds fewer than asked, the newest 5,000 notes are checked with the same rules (each distinct word once per search). No schema change; deterministic; results are still only stored notes, shown verbatim with the query words that matched. Measured on Linux x86 release build: ~0.13 s per search over 3,000 notes with ~3,000 distinct words | Applied 2026-10-09 — typo and counterpart matches can surface a loosely related note; the agent still only repeats stored text. Not yet tried on a device |
| 094 | "Talk to Doddy" control for Control Center, Lock Screen and the Action Button (iOS 18+, `ControlWidget` in the existing `DoddyWidgets` extension, behind `#available`). It runs the existing `TalkToDoddyIntent`, moved to `Shared/` so both targets compile it; app-only code is behind `DODDY_WIDGET_EXTENSION`. Replaces the separate `DoddyControls` extension idea from the unmerged `busy-mendel` branch: one extension, one intent | Applied 2026-10-09 — not compiled or tried on a device |
| 095 | **Today card + event nudges** on Home (`TODAY_STATUS.md`): rest of today from iPhone Calendar and due/overdue Reminders, only when the existing Connections switches are on and iOS allows; local nudges 10/30/60 min before a timed event, shown as set only after read-back from iOS, one per event (a new lead time replaces), moved events flagged. Everything stays on the phone; event/reminder notes are not read. Rebuilt on the merged Connections code instead of merging `upbeat-brown` | Applied 2026-10-09 — not compiled or tried on a device |

## Decisions the owner must approve before M1 (written at M0; items 6–7 since applied as defaults in M1)

1. **Narrow MVP job** — confirm capture → later retrieval (text) as the first utility, and whether first beta needs real text AI (M2) or can wait.
2. **Visual master** — Pocket Notebook vs Quiet Companion vs hybrid, and which of the two v1.3 brand ZIPs (COMPLETE vs non-COMPLETE) is the character master; human illustrator review of the art.
3. **Artwork rights/provenance** — confirm you may ship the candidate PNGs/SVGs (provenance file says AI-assisted task output, no trademark review), and approve app icon work.
4. **Typography** — system fonts only for now, or license Caveat/other display font (file binaries are deliberately excluded).
5. **Targets** — minimum iOS (17.0 proposed), device matrix (iPhone only proposed), and the Xcode version to standardise on.
6. **Text-length semantics** (ADR-016) and **tombstone vs purge** (ADR-017).
7. **Persistence choice for M1** — approve GRDB/SQLite + FTS5 (ADR-003) and which GRDB release; export formats (JSON + plain text proposed).
8. **Identity & signing** — bundle identifier, Apple developer team, and whether to commit the generated `Doddy.xcodeproj` after a first good Mac build (ADR-013).
9. **CI** — accept local-script CI only, or approve a hosted macOS runner and its cost (ADR-022).
10. **Run the Mac verification** listed in `docs/implementation/M0_REPORT.md` and record results; **M1 should not start until M0's iOS build/tests are confirmed on a Mac** (M0 exit criteria are not met without it).

## Connected Apps decisions (2026-10-09, owner prompt "Composio integration"; renumbered from 038–047 at branch consolidation)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 073 | Add a minimal backend `backend/` (Node 22, TypeScript, `node:http`, `node:sqlite`, zod, `@composio/core` 0.22.0 exact). No framework, no microservices, no vector store, no generic tool proxy. | Applied — approval pending |
| 074 | Doddy-owned API v1 and capability registry (`calendar.listEvents` only enabled; `calendar.createEvent` listed but disabled). Composio tool slugs appear only in `backend/src/providers/composio.ts` (guard-enforced). | Applied |
| 075 | Read-only is enforced twice: requested scope `calendar.events.readonly` on the auth config, and a server-side allowlist on the **granted** scopes; write-capable or unverifiable grants block reads. | Applied — needs live confirmation that Composio exposes granted scopes |
| 076 | Completion: `verifier` (Composio OAuth callback verifier + `completeAuth`) by default; `callback` mode only in `DODDY_ENV=development`. Attempts are single-use, 10-minute, nonce-bound, and re-verified server-side (status, toolkit, auth config, ownership via `userIds` filter). | Applied — verifier availability Plausible |
| 077 | App sessions: loopback dev identities (mock only) or owner-minted HMAC tokens; production live mode refused until real sign-in exists. | Applied — **owner decision needed on sign-in** |
| 078 | Calendar results are transient (15-minute expiry, cleared on disconnect/failure), never stored by app or backend; saving is one explicit tap per event, stored as `shared_content` naming Google Calendar. | Applied |
| 079 | App networking only in `DoddyApp/Infrastructure/URLSessionTransport.swift`; system auth browser only in `Features/ConnectedApps/SystemWebAuthenticator.swift`; session token in Keychain (`AfterFirstUnlockThisDeviceOnly`). | Applied |
| 080 | `CapabilityStatus.limited(note)` added; `connectedAccounts` is "Limited · Google Calendar read-only, needs a Doddy server", never "Available". | Applied |
| 081 | Composio SDK start-up npm version check disabled and API base URL pinned (constructor otherwise contacted registry.npmjs.org). | Applied |
| 082 | Usage ceilings are Doddy policies in SQLite (UTC day/month), reserved before every provider call including pages and retries; concurrency per process (single instance). Monetary cost estimated only. | Applied — owner to set real ceilings |

## Online accounts through Composio: Gmail, Drive, Notion, Todoist (2026-10-10, owner prompt "connect composio with doddy and add more tools in settings to switch on or off")

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 096 | A fixed catalog of six Doddy-named tools (`check_email`, `draft_email`, `find_drive_file`, `search_notion`, `list_todoist_tasks`, `add_todoist_task`), each with a strict input schema, hand-built Composio arguments and a normalizer that keeps a few bounded fields. No generic "run any Composio tool" route (the guard script still bans proxy and meta-tool execution). | Applied; mock-tested only |
| 097 | **Every tool has its own on/off switch, off by default, enforced on the server** (`tool_switches`), not only in the app. Connecting an account never switches tools on; disconnecting or an expired account switches that account's tools off and a reconnect does not restore them. The app's switch moves only after the server accepts it. | Applied |
| 098 | Only two write tools exist: a Gmail **draft** and a Todoist **task**. Each needs a fresh single-use approval bound to user, tool, connection and the SHA-256 of the exact validated arguments (2-minute expiry), created by `/v1/tools/prepare` after the user sees the preview and used by `/v1/tools/run` after the tap. Writes never auto-retry; a timeout reports `outcome_unknown`. | Applied |
| 099 | Gmail needs `gmail.compose` for drafts, and Google's compose scope also permits sending. Doddy has no send tool and the guard script refuses any `GMAIL_SEND`/delete/label slug, but the **grant itself is wider than the feature**. Gmail read uses `gmail.readonly`; Drive asks only `drive.metadata.readonly`. Notion and Todoist have no scope list Doddy can check. | Applied — **owner to accept or drop drafting** |
| 100 | Grants are checked at connect and again on every run (Google toolkits: every granted scope must be in the allowlist, and the tool's own scope present; unverifiable means blocked). Notion/Todoist skip this check because Composio exposes no comparable scope list. | Applied; whether Composio reports scopes is unverified |
| 101 | Results for the agent say what the provider returned and nothing more. Mock servers prefix `DEMO DATA …`, so demo data is never read out as the user's. Email gives sender, subject, time and a 140-character preview only; Notion titles only; Drive name/type/time only. Receipts and logs hold no content. | Applied |
| 102 | The agent prompt treats email, file names, Notion titles and tasks as information, never instructions (prompt-injection guard); writes still need an on-screen tap even if it were fooled. | Applied on the live dev agent |
| 103 | The live dev agent (`agent_0501…`) got the six client tools and the prompt rules on 2026-10-10 (version `agtvrsn_2801m4hqvf0qfeb8h03ts6cdethc`; previous `agtvrsn_8101m4gvnve7fdzsyqk5rjyqcfb8` is the rollback). `voice-agent/tools.json` now also lists the four Connections tools that had drifted. | Applied |
| 104 | Live mode still requires the Google Calendar pair to start; a toolkit with no auth config + pinned version shows "Not set up" instead of failing the server. | Open — decouple calendar from live mode later |
| 105 | **Voice choice, speed and expressiveness** in Settings → Voice and personality (`VoiceStyle`, DoddyCore). Fixed lists only: 6 voices already in the owner's ElevenLabs account (Doddy's own `oDULUm0GMltHA6mVZkWh` = default, Josh, Wiktor (native Polish), River, Jessica, Will), speed 0.85 / 1.0 / 1.15, stability 0.75 / 0.5 / 0.3. Sent as ElevenLabs **TTS overrides** at conversation start; only changed values are sent, defaults send nothing. Never touches the prompt, so rules and memory behaviour can't change. Mood stays a prompt dial (ADR-062). | Built. Agent side applied 2026-10-10 (owner "yes turn on"): the dev agent allows client overrides for `tts.voice_id`, `tts.speed` and `tts.stability` only, version `agtvrsn_5301m4jk5fz1edx8vz0r04hh2mcc` (rollback = set those three back to false). Not compiled on Xcode or heard on a device |
| 122 | **Voice samples bundled in the app**, not streamed: one clip per voice (`DoddyApp/Resources/VoicePreviews/voice-preview-<voice>.mp3`, ~95 KB each, ~540 KB total) saying "Hey, it's Doddy. What's on your mind? Hej, tu Doddy. Co słychać?", recorded 2026-10-10 with ElevenLabs `eleven_v4` on the owner's account (flow `UBUULfvGRXWrH1C2eV5i`). Settings → Voice and personality → "Hear <voice>" plays it with `AVAudioPlayer` at the chosen speed (rate = TTS speed); expressiveness is not applied to a recording. No network, no new data flow, no cost per play. Not played while a conversation is live (`VoiceSessionController.conversationInProgress`). | Built — not compiled or heard on a device. Re-record if the hello line or a voice changes. Library-voice licence terms (Josh, Wiktor) still to check before release (ADR-038) |
| 132 | **Nickname and starting language** (Settings → Voice and personality). Nickname: optional, `Nickname.clean` accepts only a single-line name (letters in any language, spaces, hyphens, apostrophes, full stops, ≤30 chars) and rejects anything else; when set it is one extra line inside the existing `{{doddy_personality}}` text, so **no agent change** is needed and the default text is unchanged. It is new user text sent to ElevenLabs, so it is opt-in by typing it and the footer says so. Starting language: Automatic (default, sends nothing, agent detection as before) / English / Polish, sent as the ElevenLabs `agent.language` override, which also selects that language's greeting preset. | Built. Agent side applied 2026-10-10 (owner "yes, turn on language override"): the dev agent now also allows the `agent.language` client override, version `agtvrsn_9701m4jtjj5hek1a1h6tbqb9xrvf` (rollback = set it back to false; TTS voice/speed/stability stay allowed, prompt/LLM/tools/first message stay off). Not compiled or heard on a device |
| 123 | **Hosted macOS CI** (owner approval 2026-10-10, "Cloud iOS build"): `.github/workflows/ios.yml` runs `scripts/ci.sh` on `macos-latest` with the latest stable Xcode on every pull request and on demand: pack check, guard, Swift package tests, server/backend/gateway tests, XcodeGen + `xcodebuild test` (unit + UI) on an iPhone simulator. No signing team, no secrets, no deploys; `.xcresult` kept 7 days. Uses GitHub's macOS minutes. Simulator only: device-only checks (background audio, Island on hardware, Back Tap) stay manual. | Applied |

## M2 decisions (typed assistant, 2026-10-10; renumbered from 038–050 at branch consolidation)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 108 | Provider is **OpenAI** (owner choice, 2026-10-10), behind the provider-neutral `AssistantClient`. The model id is read from `OPENAI_MODEL` and has **no default**: two sources disagreed on current ids and none was verifiable. | Applied — owner to set the model and confirm it supports Structured Outputs |
| 109 | Gateway is **Vercel Functions** (owner choice), plain ESM JavaScript with JSDoc types, **zero runtime dependencies**, `node:test` tests. Written and tested; **not deployed** (deploying creates a billable resource and needs the owner). | Applied — deploy pending |
| 110 | The typed assistant's HTTP lives only in `DoddyApp/Infrastructure/Network/AppHTTPTransport.swift`. The package has no networking; transport and credential stores are ports with fakes. Since consolidation the guard allows URLSession/URLRequest in exactly three files: this one, the voice token client and Connected Apps' `URLSessionTransport` (ADR-079). | Applied |
| 111 | Auth is **invite code → 24 h HMAC bearer token** bound to a random install id. No accounts, no profile. Removing an invite code revokes every token minted with it immediately. Not identity and not abuse-proof; App Attest/accounts are future work. | Applied — **owner to confirm for beta** |
| 112 | Notes reach the model as aliases (`n1…`), never as real ids. The response schema is strict and its `sources` enum is exactly this request's aliases. Gateway re-checks, and the **device verifies again** (`CitationVerifier`) before showing anything. A failed check discards the whole answer. | Applied |
| 113 | `store:false` is sent to OpenAI. Whether that is honoured, and OpenAI's retention/usage terms, are **not verified**. UI copy says "we ask OpenAI not to keep the reply, but we can't verify that". | Applied — legal review pending |
| 114 | Rate limiting sits behind a `RateLimiter` port; the shipped adapter is per-instance memory. It is a brake, not a spend cap. Real cap = a hard budget on the OpenAI project + a shared store before any outside tester. | Applied — **blocker for inviting others** |
| 115 | Ask Doddy is **off by default**. Turning it on shows what is sent and to whom. "Use relevant saved notes" is a separate switch. Only notes that matched the question, up to 5 / 12,000 characters, are sent, and the answer shows which ones. | Applied |
| 116 | Chats live in memory only, are never saved as notes, and a failed ask leaves the question in the box and local memory untouched. "Save this answer as a note" is **not built**. | Applied |
| 117 | Uncited answers are labelled "Not from your notes. This is a general answer." The system cannot detect a model making up a personal fact without citing, and does not claim to. | Applied — known residual risk |
| 118 | The random install id is declared in `PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy` as Device ID (app functionality, not linked, no tracking). Over-declaring is the safe direction; the App Store privacy label is an owner/legal decision. | Applied — owner to review |
| 119 | Ask smoke tests added to `LaunchSmokeTests` (the Microphone assertion fix was also made on the main line). | Applied — NOT RUN |
| 120 | Gmail, Google Calendar and EventKit were a proposal on the M2 branch (`docs/architecture/CONNECTED_APPS_PROPOSAL.md`). | **Superseded**: the main line built Connections (EventKit, contacts) and Connected Apps (ADR-073–082, 096–105); the proposal is kept for reference only |
| 133 | **What leaves this iPhone** (Settings → Privacy): one row per destination (ElevenLabs, Open-Meteo/Wikipedia via ElevenLabs, voice token server, your Doddy server, Ask Doddy gateway), each saying what goes and when, built by `DataFlow.rows` from the current settings (remember conversations, introduction answers, token server, connections on, Pause all, Doddy server address + token, Ask Doddy and its notes switch). Off destinations are listed as "Not sent right now" with the reason, plus a "Stays on this iPhone" list. It restates the Privacy paragraph row by row; a comment in SettingsView asks to change both together. | Applied — app UI NOT RUN |
| 134 | **Pause all connections** (Settings → Connections, and a card on the conversation screen): one switch, `ConnectionPause.preferenceKey`, checked before every tool call. While on, every call that would reach another app or account (calendar, reminders, contacts, timers, maps, shortcuts, messages, Gmail, Drive, Notion, Todoist) gets a `paused:` reply and does nothing; notes still work. Home → Today stops reading Calendar and Reminders. Per-connection switches and iPhone permissions are left alone, so turning it off restores what was on. Event nudges already scheduled stay scheduled. Paused calls show in What Doddy did as "Didn't happen · Connections were paused." | Applied — app UI NOT RUN |
| 135 | "What Doddy did" (Settings → Connections): every voice tool call is logged at the one place its reply is sent, so the entry can never say more than the agent was told (`ActivityEntry(request:reply:)`). Look-ups and changes both; online-account entries keep the tool name only, no email subjects, file names or task text. JSON in Application Support/Activity, newest first, 30 days / 300 entries, cleared with one tap (clearing never touches other apps). A reminder Doddy wasn't sure about gets **Check in Reminders**, which looks for its `ReminderKey` marker and adds nothing. Requests still waiting on a card when the conversation ends are logged as not done. No crash-recovery state yet. | Applied — app UI NOT RUN |
| 136 | Reminders can't be added twice: each reminder Doddy adds carries a marker URL (`doddy://reminder/<key>`, key = FNV-1a of folded text + due minute/day, `ReminderKey`). Before adding, the app looks for an incomplete reminder with that key and, if found, adds nothing and tells the agent it was already there; after adding, it reads back by the marker. A `create_reminder` due in the past is refused as invalid. Covers A-22 for repeated requests and "try again" after an unconfirmed add. Not done: a durable "What Doddy did" log and crash recovery (proposal and code on commit c646ee3, built on old main). | **Superseded by ADR-126** (the main line's M4 reliable write, same marker idea); kept for the record |
| 169 | What Doddy did survives a crash: when the user confirms a change (save note, add event or reminder, start timer, online-account write), an "In progress" entry is written to disk before the change runs; the verified result replaces it under the same id. At launch any entry still in progress becomes "Not sure" with "Doddy closed before this finished. Check before asking again" (`ActivityLog.recoverInterrupted`); an interrupted reminder gets Check in Reminders. Opening Maps, Shortcuts or a draft is not logged in advance. Closes the "crash recovery" gap left in ADR-125. | Applied — app UI NOT RUN |
| 121 | Consolidation (owner chose this M2 branch over `busy-hawking` and `friendly-sagan`, 2026-10-10): the shipping capability catalog is `v2WithAssistant` = V2 with Connected Apps plus `textAssistant`; privacy flags stay `v1Voice` (same flags as M2's). Home gets an **Ask Doddy** button under Talk to Doddy in the Codex layout; Settings gets the Ask Doddy section with copy that names the gateway as a separate server from the Connected Apps server. The other two M2 branches are not merged. | Applied — unbuilt |
| 131 | **Island Mute/End handoff fix attempt.** Red check in `NATIVE_BASELINE_REPORT.md`: the system Unmute tap on the iOS 27 simulator left the fixture "Fixture muted". The fixture label follows the `isMuted` that its registered handler toggles, so the app-side handler most likely never ran (the `@State island` change in `9ebd4f8` cannot explain that). Likely cause: the intents in `Shared/` are compiled into the app **and** the widget extension (`project.yml`), whose `VoiceActivityActions.shared` is a separate object with nil handlers. Apple's iOS 27 docs say an intent runs "using any available target" by default ([IntentExecutionTargets](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/appintents/intentexecutiontargets)). Fix: `EndDoddyConversationIntent`, `ToggleDoddyMuteIntent` and `TalkToDoddyIntent` set `allowedExecutionTargets` to `.main` on iOS 27+. The declaration is behind `#if compiler(>=6.4)`, so Xcode 16/26 builds keep the old routing, which Apple documents as app-process for `LiveActivityIntent` ([interactivity docs](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/widgetkit/adding-interactivity-to-widgets-and-live-activities)). No Darwin-notification or app-group relay was added; that is the fallback if this is not enough. | Applied — **not compiled, not run on a simulator or device**; Mac must re-run `MemoryBaselineTests/testNativeIslandOfflineFixtureCanStartMuteAndEnd` |
| 137 | **Linux CI job** `core` in `.github/workflows/ios.yml`: runs `scripts/ci.sh --core-only` (pack check, guards, agent spec, Swift package tests, token server, backend and gateway tests) in the official `swift:6.1-noble` container on `ubuntu-24.04`, next to the macOS `ci` job (ADR-123). Gives every PR a real signal while no hosted Mac is assigned (the macOS job has failed with no runner since it was added) and is cheaper per minute. It never claims the iOS app was built: the script prints NOT RUN for it. | Applied — not yet run on GitHub |
| 124 | **Shared rate limiter** behind the existing `RateLimiter` port: `SharedRateLimiter` counts epoch-aligned fixed windows in a Redis-compatible store over its REST API (Upstash Redis REST; Vercel Marketplace Redis/KV exposes the same), `INCR` + `EXPIRE` in one `MULTI/EXEC`, plain `fetch`, zero dependencies. Env: `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL`/`_TOKEN` or `KV_REST_API_URL`/`_TOKEN`. Store keys are HMAC-ed with the server secret (no device ids, invite tags or IPs in the store). **Fail closed:** store configured but unreachable → `503 providerUnavailable`, never a memory fallback; half-configured → `503 notConfigured`; `REQUIRE_SHARED_LIMITS=1` makes a missing store `notConfigured`. No store at all → per-instance memory (dev) with a startup warning. Refines ADR-114. | Built and tested against a fake store only — **owner to create the store** (new service, approval) and set `REQUIRE_SHARED_LIMITS=1` |
| 125 | **Hard daily spend cap** (`SpendCap` port): before each OpenAI call reserve a worst-case estimate ((prompt bytes + 500) × input price + 800 × output price) on two scopes, everyone and this install, per UTC day (`DAILY_BUDGET_USD`, default 1.00; `DAILY_BUDGET_PER_INSTALL_USD`, default 0.25); after the call replace it with provider-reported tokens × price (4xx: released; timeout/5xx/no usage: estimate kept). Over cap → new wire code `dailyLimitReached` (429, "Doddy's daily limit is reached; try again tomorrow.", retry after next UTC midnight); the app maps it to `AssistantError.dailyLimitReached`, not auto-retried. Prices come from `OPENAI_PRICE_INPUT_PER_MTOK_USD`/`_OUTPUT_` because no model price could be verified; the defaults (10 / 40 USD per 1M tokens) are pessimistic placeholders, not real prices. A hard limit on the OpenAI project remains the second line of defence. | Built and tested against a fake store only — **owner to set real prices, the budget, and the OpenAI project limit** |
| 126 | **M4 Reminders: reliable write** (`docs/implementation/M4_REMINDERS_STATUS.md`). (1) **No duplicates, idempotent recovery:** before writing, Doddy reads the open reminders on the iPhone and skips the write if the same one is there, matched by an idempotency key (FNV-1a 64 of normalized title + due wall clock, stored in the reminder's URL field as `doddy://reminder/v1-…`; the tool call id is not part of it because a retry gets a new id) or by the same normalized title and due. That includes the user's own identical reminders. A failed save is followed by a fresh read (found → `added`, absent → `failed`, unreadable → `unknown`); `added` only after a read-back with matching title, due and zone. Reads time out after 10 s (before the write → nothing written). (2) **Dates and zones:** `create_reminder` accepts only `YYYY-MM-DD[THH:MM]` local time, no zone suffix; bare times, words, past times, and times skipped or repeated by a clock change are sent back to the agent as a question (`failed: not added yet …`), never guessed; the time is rechecked at the tap. A timed reminder is saved with the phone's zone explicitly and the card/receipt show it; day-only stays floating. A receipt card shows the read-back result after the tap. New on-device read: title, due, list, URL of open reminders (not stored, not sent). | Applied — NOT RUN on device; live agent prompt/tool wording applied 2026-10-10 (ADR-129) |

| 127 | **Calendar times use the reminder date rules** (follow-up to ADR-126). `VoiceLocalDate.parse` read `2026-10-12T18:30+02:00` as 18:00 (it dropped whatever followed a non-numeric piece), so `create_calendar_event` could put an event at the wrong time without saying so; `read_calendar` had the same parser. It is removed. `ReminderDueRules` is now shared: `wallClock(text, calendar)` is the strict reading (only `YYYY-MM-DD[THH:MM[:SS]]`, local time, no zone suffix or offset, no words or bare time, no rolled-over date such as Feb 31, no time the clocks skip or repeat), and `check` for reminders is `wallClock` plus "not already passed". `create_calendar_event` runs `start` and `end` through `wallClock`; anything it refuses is not shown as a card but sent back as `failed: not added yet: <problem>. Ask the user <question>, then call create_calendar_event again…` (`VoiceToolReply.clarification(_:tool:)`; the reminder text is unchanged). The `added` / `declined` / `unknown` / `failed` contract is unchanged; the existing prompt line on "not added yet" already covers it. A past start is **not** refused for events (logging something that happened is legitimate; the card shows the day) — owner may choose otherwise. `read_calendar` uses `wallClock` too and keeps its `.invalid` replies. Dates are read as Gregorian in the phone's zone even if the phone uses another calendar (before, a Buddhist-calendar phone would have read 2026 as 1483 CE). Tool wording in `voice-agent/tools.json` (calendar description, `start`/`end`) updated to match. | Applied — DoddyCore tests on Linux; app call site changed but NOT built (no Xcode here) and NOT RUN on device; live agent tool wording applied 2026-10-10 (ADR-129) |
| 128 | **Calendar events: no past start** (owner decision 2026-10-10, replaces the "past start is not refused" part of ADR-127). `create_calendar_event` now applies the full reminder rules: a timed start that has passed, or an all-day event on a day already over, is not shown as a card; the agent gets `failed: not added yet: <time> has already passed (it is now …) … then call create_calendar_event again`. Checked again when the user taps Add (`CalendarEventDraft.passed(now:calendar:)`), since the card can wait while the start goes by; nothing is written then either. An all-day event for today is fine all day. `read_calendar` still accepts past days (reading the past is legitimate). `start` parameter wording in `voice-agent/tools.json` says it must be later than now. | Applied — DoddyCore tests on Linux; app call site syntax-checked only (no Xcode), NOT RUN on device |
| 129 | **Live agent synced; owner's opening line kept** (owner decision 2026-10-10). Applied to agent `agent_0501m4et27vyeawrwqfdxxfszfa6` with the owner's approval: the `create_calendar_event` and `create_reminder` tool descriptions and parameters from `voice-agent/tools.json` (ADR-126, ADR-127, ADR-128) and the one Calendar-and-reminder rules line ("already in Reminders" / "not added yet") in the prompt. Before writing, the live prompt was diffed against `instructions.md`: the only other difference was a dashboard edit of the opening line (Doddy as an "online companion" that, asked whether it is human or an AI, should "say vague answer"; the repo said "You are an AI, not a person … say so plainly"). The owner chose to keep the live line, so `instructions.md` now carries it verbatim and the rest of the live prompt was left as it was. New live version `agtvrsn_5801m4jtvazveprbj3znhn66j4tt` (previous `agtvrsn_9701m4jtjj5hek1a1h6tbqb9xrvf`). **Conflict left open:** this line contradicts `docs/design/BRAND_GUIDELINES.md` ("Doddy is an AI, not a person", say it plainly), `docs/product/PERSONALITY_SETTINGS.md` ("Honest. An AI, not a person") and the AI-disclosure note in `docs/implementation/V1_VOICE_STATUS.md`, and may not meet the EU AI Act transparency duty for systems that talk with people (see `docs/security/PRIVACY_GDPR_AI_ACT.md`); those docs were not changed. | Applied live; AI-disclosure conflict resolved by ADR-191 (opening line no longer kept) |

Connecting Google Calendar/Gmail is a separate OAuth "connection", not a login. | Owner-approved (deferred) |
