## Chat with Doddy on the web (2026-10-24)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 657 | *(ADR-604 on its branch `claude/exciting-carson-08x2pk`; renumbered when it was merged into the default branch on 28 Oct 2026, because the default branch's 604 is the island fix. Its code and docs still say ADR-604.)* **Doddy Web's chat looks like ChatGPT in Doddy's colours and reads the whole account, not only kept memory** (owner, 24 Oct 2026, in Polish: an imitation of ChatGPT in the Doddy portal "where the analytics are", a chat with Doddy's memory that "knows about you, is connected to the same things as you are in the app", where you can ask analytical questions about your account, "how much, how he talks", "like ChatGPT, but in Doddy's colours"). Widens ADR-603's chat. <ul><li>**Page:** `/chat` (Ask Doddy's `/ask` redirects there), second in the menu, no Soon marker because it answers from the account today. The list of chats on the left, the conversation in a centred column, a round message box, suggested questions on a new chat, sources under each answer, "What I can see", Copy, Answer again, Stop; a drawer on phones; light and dark from the website's tokens. Home links to it.</li><li>**What it reads per message** (`backend/src/web/chatContext.ts`, caller's own user id): kept notes and conversations as before, plus five account parts the answer can cite by name: `logbook` (Logbook's days added up per period in `logbookDigest.ts`, counts only), `talk` (counts over kept conversations from plain columns), `apps`, `documents`, `activity`. All as JSON data; none in the instructions. Today's date in the browser's time zone. Ten earlier turns (was six).</li><li>**Nothing kept no longer means no chat:** with the account to read, a question is answered (and counted against the daily caps) even when no notes or conversations are kept or the memory key is unset; that part then says "off" or "not set up".</li><li>**`GET /v1/web/chat/context`:** whether the chat model is set up, the switches and counts, connected account names, document and receipt counts. No content.</li><li>**Chats are kept in this browser only** (localStorage), tied to the browser session, deleted at Sign out, at the session's end and when another sign-in opens; never on the server.</li><li>**New to Anthropic:** document names, receipt summaries, account names and statuses, Logbook's counts. Privacy draft 5.13 updated.</li><li>**Open for the owner:** chats on the server instead of the browser; sending every part or only what a question needs. `docs/product/WEB_CHAT.md`.</li></ul> | Built and tested (server and web, end to end in Chromium with the demo model); not run against the real model; not deployed. |
| 658 | **Home control by voice through HomeKit** (owner, 28 Oct 2026: "connection to Apple HomeKit so someone can set up Doddy by command", then "Build"). <ul><li>**What it does:** `control_home` turns a light, outlet or fan on or off, or runs a scene, in the person's Home. A model only proposes words; `HomeCommandRules` (DoddyCore) resolves them to one exact accessory or scene, never picks between two matches, and the card shows HomeKit's own name for it. Nothing runs until the tap (ASK-01 `.ownPayloadCard`).</li><li>**Never:** locks, garage doors, security systems, cameras, doorbells and thermostats are refused in code; a scene is offered only when every action in it writes to a light, outlet or fan, so a scene can't unlock a door through Doddy; the guard keeps HomeKit in `HomeKitClient.swift` and bans triggers, camera streams, accessory setup and lock, door and security characteristics.</li><li>**Honest results:** a power change is written, then read back; only a matching read is `changed:`. A scene is confirmed by HomeKit's own acknowledgement. No answer is `unknown:` and is never retried. The reply prefixes are the ones the activity log already reads.</li><li>**Privacy:** the name of what was asked and whether it worked go to Doddy's voice in that conversation; the home itself is never sent or kept. The HomeKit prompt only appears after Allow is tapped. Needs the HomeKit entitlement, so a paid Apple team; the free-team build has none.</li><li>**Not done:** the live ElevenLabs agent (`control_home` is `not_synced`, needs the owner's yes to the exact text); the prompt in nine languages is a machine draft.</li></ul> | Ran on Linux: voice agent spec (101 tools), guard, platform specs, string catalogs. **NOT COMPILED:** `HomeCommand.swift`, `HomeKitClient.swift` and every Swift edit (no Swift or Xcode in the session); `make test-core`, the Mac job and a device are the first runs. HomeKit calls (`HMHomeManager`, `writeValue`, `executeActionSet`) written from Apple's API without the SDK. |

## Leaflets after a live check (2026-10-28)

| ADR | Decision | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 659 | *(ADR-657 on its branch `claude/deal-finder-fixes`; renumbered on 28 Oct 2026 because the default branch took 657 for the web chat and #149 holds 658.)* **Leaflet fixes after a live check of the four chains (28 Oct 2026): Lidl reads again, a leaflet read in part says so, "masło" no longer finds "masa", and the best value per kg comes first** (owner, 28 Oct 2026, in Polish, "Tak" to fixing points 1 to 4 of a review of Deal Finder). A read of the chains' live pages from a cloud session on 28 Oct found: (1) **every Lidl read failed** as `network`: lidl.pl's leaflet list answers with about 20.5 KiB of response headers and Node's default limit is 16 KiB (`HPE_HEADER_OVERFLOW`); the polite fetcher now accepts 64 KiB (`MAX_HEADER_BYTES`, `fetcher.ts`), for leaflets and shop pages alike. (2) **Biedronka's leaflet had 97 image pages** against the 40-page cap (`DEALS_MAX_PAGES`), so pages 41 to 97 were never read while the row said `checked` (or `failed` when nothing matched in the first 40). A chain row now carries `pages: {read, of}` whenever fewer pages were read than were worth reading (the cap, the time limit, an image that couldn't be fetched or read), on `checked` and `failed` rows only, counting the pages where only an image read finds offers (Biedronka's, Lidl's pages that name an item; not Dino's, whose pages only confirm offers its data already gave, flagged `check` until then); the app shows "Read in part" (or, for `failed` with some pages read, how many were read with nothing in them) and the agent's data says "Read only in part, so a cheaper offer may be on the pages not read". The cap itself is unchanged: reading all of a large image leaflet within the 25 s answer needs a separate decision (in the background, or once a week with a lawyer's yes, since ADR-609 keeps nothing). (3) **Matching:** a stem plus extra letters counted as the same word, so "masło" matched Dino's "Masa makowa" and "Masa krówkowa" at 0.75 (above the 0.6 threshold); now only a diminutive tail (`k`, `ek`, `ik`, `yk`: ser/serek, jaja/jajka) counts, and a stem that the other word merely extends (kawa/kawowy, mięso/mięsny, jogurt/jogurtowy) does not match. (4) **Order:** offers were sorted by pack price, so a small pack beat a kilo one. Now best value first: the lowest price per kg, l or piece in the unit most of the item's offers have (kg, then l, then piece on a tie), then the packs whose size wasn't read, by price; each chain's cheapest pack still makes the six, because the basket (`BasketSolver`, unchanged) buys one pack of each item. DoddyCore's `BasketSolver.bestValue` applies the same rule, so the screen's first offer and the agent's "lowest price per kg at …" agree even with an older server. **Checked that the Biedronka redirect still works:** `/pl/w-tym-tygodniu` reaches the press page in two hops (an earlier note in the conversation said otherwise; it was wrong). **Not changed:** the voice tool's description (`voice-agent/tools.json`, live agent untouched), the leaflet cap, the vision reader, what is stored (nothing). **Seen and left:** without Haiku's pick, Rossmann's search for "masło" returns body butters ("masło shea"), which the code alone can't tell from butter. | Linux, 28 Oct: messaging server 409/409 with Postgres 16 (58 leaflet tests: new matching cases from Dino's 44/2026 leaflet, a leaflet read in part, best value with a pack without a size, and each chain's cheapest pack kept); DoddyCore 2,174/2,174 (pages decoding, the partial lines for the agent, best value); `scripts/typecheck_app_logic.sh` (34 files), string catalogs `--check`, the forbidden-capability guard, voice agent spec; `swiftc -parse` of `DealsView.swift`. **Live, from a cloud session:** with the fix the Lidl list answers 200 and four current leaflets are read (20 pages worth reading for "masło" and "kawa mielona"); Dino, Rossmann and Biedronka's leaflet list answer as before. **NOT COMPILED:** `DealsView.swift` (no Xcode here); no real Haiku read (no Anthropic key here), no device, not deployed. |
| 660 | *(ADR-658 on its branch `claude/deal-finder-aldi-zabka`; renumbered on 28 Oct 2026 because #149 holds 658.)* **Aldi and Żabka are read leaflet chains** (owner, 28 Oct 2026, in Polish: "Ok" to starting with point 1 of the proposal in `docs/research/2026-10-28/research_more_deal_sources.md`, "Aldi i Żabka jako nowe gazetki"). Both were checked live through Doddy's own fetcher on 28 Oct. **Aldi** (`chains/aldi.ts`): one fetch of `www.aldi.pl/oferty.html` (robots.txt allows it); its Next.js payload carries this week's offers as Aldi's own data (66 that day: name, pack, price, price per kg, promotion text, regular price, the 30-day text, dates), so no model reads anything. The mechanics are written by the code: "przy zakupie N szt." is a minimum quantity; "N+M GRATIS" a multi-buy; "co druga sztuka" carries the **second** item's price (2,99 zł for a 36,99 zł jar), so the offer's price is the price of one when buying two, `ceil((regular + second) / 2)` in grosze, with "przy zakupie 2 szt." and "druga sztuka 2,99 zł" as conditions, and an offer without the regular price is left out rather than shown at the second item's price; "… najniższa cena z 30 dni" is `lowest30d`, "… sprzed pierwszej obniżki" (perishables) the regular price; "Cena za kg" is a 1 kg size; Aldi's printed unit price is kept for the cross-check only when the pack size is known (and never for "co druga"). **Żabka** (`chains/zabka.ts`): the leaflet list on `www.zabka.pl/gazetka-promocyjna/` names each leaflet with its dates; `gazetka.zabka.pl/{slug}/spreads.json` (Żabka's leaflet site on Publitas) gives each page's own text and its image on `view.publitas.com`; neither Publitas host has a robots.txt (404, no rules under RFC 9309; Rossmann's Publitas site, which disallows every robot, stays unread). The text picks the pages worth reading and the reader reads prices from the image, as for Lidl; "z aplikacją Żappka" is Żabka's app condition. **Alcohol:** Aldi's offers by name and category, Żabka's pages carrying the legal beer warning ("… czystego alkoholu …"; 12 of 72 that day) are never read. Żabka leaves the link-only list (now Kaufland, Carrefour, Auchan, Netto). **Order of release:** an older server refuses `aldi`, and an older app refuses `aldi` from the agent, so the server is deployed first, then the app, then the voice tool's new text (`voice-agent/README.md`), with the owner's yes. **Privacy:** Aldi gets one page request from the server, nothing else; Żabka's page images go to Anthropic with the item names, like Lidl's; nothing that identifies the user goes to either. | Linux, 28 Oct: messaging server 415/415 with Postgres 16 (64 leaflet tests: Aldi's mechanics from a trimmed copy of the page, alcohol left out, a missing payload, a block; Żabka's list and pages, beer pages left out, only this week's leaflet read, a search with both chains end to end); DoddyCore 2,174/2,174 (chain lists, names, the ask card's shops); voice agent spec. **Live, from a cloud session, no model:** Aldi 64 offers; Żabka 60 pages with text (12 beer pages left out). **Not checked:** a real Haiku read of a Żabka page, a device, a deploy; `find_deals`'s new text is not on the live agent. |
| 661 | **Leaflets, stage 1 of the research's order: the real price, promotions without a price, "Przed wyjściem", my loyalty apps, my list, trading Sundays** (owner, 28 Oct 2026, in Polish: "Tak zrób wszystko" to the order in `docs/research/2026-10-28/research_deals_better.md` §6). **Server** (`src/deals/`): each offer carries `flags[]` (app, card, deposit, stock, selected_stores, not_a_cut), `effective` (the price of one piece under the mechanic, the code's arithmetic rounded up to the grosz), `cut` (the real reduction against the leaflet's own 30-day lowest price, Omnibus; never against a "regular" price, never for a multi-buy; `not_a_cut` when the 30-day low isn't above) and `limit`; the reader keeps `second_for`, `nth_discount`, `deposit`, `stock` and `selected_stores` and reads promotions printed without a price (`items[].promotions[]`, a mechanic and a range, never a price); the order ranks by the price per kg under the mechanic with 5% added per thing the price asks; the reader's system prompt is cached; an oversized or failed page image is asked for once more after 2 s; Aldi's "co druga" is the price of one jar with `second_for`; `scripts/leaflet-score.mjs` scores a run against hand labels. **DoddyCore:** the new fields decode tolerantly (a value that doesn't make sense is dropped); `BasketSolver` costs each line for the quantity in the user's words and leaves out prices that need an app the user said they don't use (`DealsPrograms`); `ShoppingCalendar` (closed Sundays, trading Sundays as amended from 2025, public holidays with 24 December, Holy Saturday until 14:00, Żabka never called closed); `DealsChecklist`; `DealsVariants` (one question, only when an item's offers are of different kinds); `DealsListItems`; the agent's answer says the per-piece price, the real cut or that it isn't one, not on yet, a last day on a closed day, the promotions, the kinds, what was left out for an app and "before going". **App:** "Z mojej listy", "My loyalty apps" (stored on the iPhone, `deals.programsNotUsed`), the full price line, promotions, a kind filter; the basket with quantities, line notes and "Before you go", shared as text. **Not built here, and why:** the weekly check and the unit-price watch (stage 3) need push and a lawyer on scheduled reads; price history needs a lawyer; the zoomed second read waits for a scored run with an API key. | Built on `claude/deals-stage1`. Linux: messaging server 425/425 with Postgres, DoddyCore 2,191/2,191, the app-logic type-check (34 files), string catalogs, the forbidden-capability guard, the voice agent spec. **Not checked:** the SwiftUI screens compile only on the Mac job; nothing on a device; no real Haiku read of a promotion; `find_deals`'s text on the live agent is unchanged (owner's yes needed for any change). |
| 662 | **Leaflets, stage 2 on the server: screen every page of an image-only leaflet, then read only what fits; categories, with similar products** (owner, 28 Oct 2026: "Tak zrób wszystko" to `docs/research/2026-10-28/research_deals_better.md` §6, stage 2). **Screening:** Biedronka's pages (strategy `all`) go to Haiku twenty at a time (≤90,000 visual tokens a call, six calls at once), asked only which images could hold each item or a range promotion for it (`SCREEN_SYSTEM`, structured output, cached); the flagged pages are read in full, then their neighbours when there's room, from the images already fetched (each page fetched once, held for the request only). Up to `DEALS_SCREEN_PAGES` (default 120, 0 = off) are screened, so a 97-page leaflet is looked at whole instead of 40 pages; a page screened and not flagged counts as checked; a batch that fails is read page by page as before; a model without `screenPages` reads as before. `leaflet-eval.mjs --screen` and the scorer's screen recall measure it; it is not measured yet (target ≥95%). **Categories:** `src/deals/categories.ts`, about 160 categories in families, written by hand (no database copied); the same category or a family's general word matches, another category in the family is `similar` (`items[].similar[]`, at most 3, never the item, never in the basket), another family is no match (fixes "masło shea" for "masło" at Rossmann without the pick). A chain's data products of the item's family count as similar even when their words don't match. **DoddyCore and the app:** `similar` decodes apart; the screen and the agent say "similar, not the same". **Not built, and why:** the reader naming a category itself and the zoomed second read wait for a scored run with an API key; Open Food Facts' taxonomy was not used (licence attribution and the API's 15 reads a minute). | Built on `claude/deals-stage2` (on top of ADR-661). ## The owner's freshest build (2026-10-29)

| ADR | Decision | Status |

| 667 | **Light appearance by default, Doddy outlined in white in dark mode, and the free-team project builds the app alone** (owner, 29 Oct 2026, after building the default branch: "stary Dodi… defaultowy ciemny ekran"; then "daj mi najświeższy update z najświeższymi wszystkimi ustawieniami"). The three changes of `claude/welcome-dark-no-plate` (23 Oct) that never reached the default branch: `AppearancePreference` defaults to light until the person picks another in Settings › Appearance; in dark mode the character art keeps its colours with a thin white outline instead of a cream plate; `project-iphone.yml` leaves out the widgets and Save to Doddy, so a Personal team signs only the app; and (owner, 29 Oct: "make sure there is light colour by default") the launch screen is the light canvas `#F5EFE2` in both appearances (`LaunchBackground` colour, `UILaunchScreen` written in `Config/Doddy-Info.plist`, generation off in `project.yml`), so a phone in dark mode doesn't open on black. Every Doddy picture that `CharacterArtImage` draws (Welcome, the opening scene, Home's pictures) is a transparent PNG, checked on 29 Oct, so the white outline shows the figure, not a box. Ported by cherry-pick onto `claude/magical-cray-4299gg` together with the merges of Following (ADR-668, 669) and home control (#149, ADR-658). Not taken from that branch: its Messages tapbacks (`v18_message_reactions`), which conflict with the newer Messages and its database. **Not compiled here** (no Swift in the session); needs a Mac build. | Built on a branch, not compiled |
| 632 | **The Action Button talks in the Dynamic Island again; ADR-593 is reversed** (owner, 24 Oct 2026: "Action button talk to Doddy opens app not Dynamic Island :("). <ul><li>**Why it opened the app:** since ADR-593 both controls (`TalkToDoddyControl`, kind `com.bienos.doddy2.talk`, and `TalkInIslandControl`, kind `com.bienos.doddy2.talk-island`) ran `TalkToDoddyIntent` (`openAppWhenRun = true`). The island path (`TalkButtonIntent`, `TalkInIsland`) was still in the code, unwired.</li><li>**Now:** both controls run `TalkButtonIntent` again, as ADR-579 had it: a press starts the conversation in the Dynamic Island without opening Doddy, a second press (2 s or more later) ends it, a bounce is ignored. When something only the app can fix is missing (terms, microphone never allowed, voice not set up, the Face ID lock, Live Activities off, or iOS refusing the background microphone), iOS asks "Open Doddy?" with the reason. Both kinds kept, so a button already set to either gets the island after the update.</li><li>**No Settings choice** (ADR-579's picker was dropped in the 23 Oct merge): `TalkButton.press` now always uses `.dynamicIsland` and no longer reads `talkLaunchMode`, so a value stored by an ADR-579 build, which the user can no longer see or change, can't keep the app opening.</li><li>**Unchanged:** the App Shortcut "Talk to Doddy" (Siri, Back Tap, the Shortcuts app, and Action Button › **Shortcut**) still opens the app: it has to stay an iOS 17 type (ADR-577). The Home Screen widget opens the app too. The guide in Ways to open Doddy › Action Button now says Controls talks in the island and Shortcut opens Doddy (English only, like the rest of that page).</li></ul> `docs/implementation/ACTION_BUTTON_ISLAND.md`. | `TalkButtonIntent` and `TalkInIsland` are the code hosted Mac CI built and tested with the controls on 22 Oct ([run 39786285579](https://github.com/Bienos/doddy.2/actions/runs/39786285579), `4ac171d`); since then only comments changed there, plus the fixed mode. Linux: `build_strings_catalog.py --check` PASS. **Hosted Mac CI [run 40107564905](https://github.com/Bienos/doddy.2/actions/runs/40107564905) on `f9a90fbc`, 24 Oct:** the app, widget extension and tests build; unit tests pass; 61 of 62 UI tests pass (1 skipped). The one failure, `MemoryJourneyTests.testDarkLargestTextKeepsTextRoutesUsable` ("home.menu.memories" not found in Home's ••• menu at the largest text size), is not this change's: it fails the same way on the default branch (run 40105647967, `f79a4bf`) and on `claude/notifications-sound-timers` after its attempted fix (run 40108709445, `b16d823`). **NOT VERIFIED on a device:** the 16 Action Button rows, above all whether iOS lets the microphone start from a cold background start (device check A, ADR-577). |
| 665 | **Home's island extension is a thin strip at the island's own height, not a block hanging under it; the listening line is clearer** (owner, 29 Oct 2026, in Polish, after seeing a picture of Home while Doddy talks: "czemu tak, to miało być na cienkim pasku chyba, weź to popraw, zrób mock-up", then on the mock-up "lepsze, a zrób lepiej widoczne jak słucha"). The 25 Oct mock-up ("Doddy: talking strip", board A) drew the island as a 37 pt pill that pushes out to the sides; ADR-596 and ADR-654 built a black shape that hung 30 to 56 pt below the camera instead. `IslandExtension` now draws a capsule over the Dynamic Island, 37 pt tall, its top 48 pt above the bottom of the top inset (11 pt at a 59 pt inset), 260 pt wide while he hears you and springing out to 330 pt while he talks (8 pt more on each burst); the G1 aurora lines and the glow of ADR-654 and the real voice level of ADR-655 are kept, drawn on each side of the camera (126 pt plus 4 on each side left empty, strongest a little way out from it, fading to nothing 12 pt from the ends). Listening is clearer: its level is 0.5 (was 0.16), which also gives it a faint glow. Muted stays grey and still at 260 pt. Home's header no longer moves down while live (8 pt, was 66); the status bar stays hidden while live, as both widths cover the clock and the battery. Drawn only on iPhones with a Dynamic Island (a top inset of 55 pt or more): **iPhones with a notch no longer get anything**, as a notch can't grow to the sides (they had the hanging block).
